                                                qk=Cz[-24415]or zr(28778,-24415,110428)
                                            else
                                                qk=10938
                                            end
                                        end
                                    elseif qk>22656 then

                                    else
                                        return at[5]
                                    end
                                elseif qk>26540 then
                                    at[4]=at[2]
                                    if at[3]~=at[3]then
                                        qk=22656
                                    else
                                        qk=Cz[1504]or zr(49108,1504,34092)
                                    end
                                else
                                    at[5]='';
                                    at[3],qk,at[1],at[2]=(#km-1)+162,65491,1,162
                                end
                            until qk==19731
